theorem CerbND.runNDFuel.eq_def :
∀ {a info err cs st : Type} (fuel : Nat) (m : ndM a info err cs st) (st0 : st),
  CerbND.runNDFuel fuel m st0 =
    match fuel with
    | 0 =>
      panicWithPosWithDecl "CerbND" "CerbND.runNDFuel" 95 4
        "CerbND.runNDFuel: ND-tree depth budget exhausted — this is FUEL EXHAUSTION, not an empty behavior set (see CerbND.lean header; budget: ndDefaultFuel)"
    | fuel.succ =>
      match m with
      | ND m_act =>
        match m_act st0 with
        | (NDactive result, st') => [(Active result, [], st')]
        | (NDkilled reason, st') => [(Killed st' reason, [], st')]
        | (NDnd _info branches, st') =>
          List.foldl
            (fun acc x =>
              match x with
              | (fst, branch) => CerbND.runNDFuel fuel branch st' ++ acc)
            [] branches
        | (NDguard _info _constraint continuation, st') => CerbND.runNDFuel fuel continuation st'
        | (NDbranch _info _constraint left right, st') =>
          CerbND.runNDFuel fuel left st' ++ CerbND.runNDFuel fuel right st'
        | (NDstep _info branches, st') =>
          List.foldl
            (fun acc x =>
              match x with
              | (fst, branch) => CerbND.runNDFuel fuel branch st' ++ acc)
            [] branches
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Audit.allowedAxioms :
List Name

List Name

def CerberusHeapLang.Audit.boundaryModules :
List Name

inductive CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh :
CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Int → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell → Prop
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.alloc :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    ∃ al,
      σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
        al.base = c.addr ∧
          al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) ∧
            al.ty = some c.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.bytes :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ c.addr (CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) = c.bytes
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.casesOn :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {id : Int} →
    {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
      {motive : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → Sort u} →
        (t : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
          ((dead : σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false) →
              (alloc :
                  ∃ al,
                    σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
                      al.base = c.addr ∧
                        al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) ∧
                          al.ty = some c.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable) →
                (nonAtomic : CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy c.ty = false) →
                  (len : c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) →
                    (bytes : CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ c.addr (CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) = c.bytes) →
                      (dec_indep :
                          ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                            CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty c.bytes =
                              CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c) →
                        motive ⋯) →
            motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.dead :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.dec_indep :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
      CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty c.bytes = CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.len :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.mk :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false →
    (∃ al,
        σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
          al.base = c.addr ∧
            al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) ∧
              al.ty = some c.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable) →
      CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy c.ty = false →
        c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty →
          CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ c.addr (CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) = c.bytes →
            (∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty c.bytes =
                  CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c) →
              CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.nonAtomic :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy c.ty = false
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.rec :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {id : Int} →
    {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
      {motive : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → Sort u} →
        ((dead : σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false) →
            (alloc :
                ∃ al,
                  σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
                    al.base = c.addr ∧
                      al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) ∧
                        al.ty = some c.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable) →
              (nonAtomic : CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy c.ty = false) →
                (len : c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) →
                  (bytes : CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ c.addr (CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) = c.bytes) →
                    (dec_indep :
                        ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                          CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty c.bytes =
                            CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c) →
                      motive ⋯) →
          (t : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) → motive t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.recOn :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {id : Int} →
    {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
      {motive : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → Sort u} →
        (t : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
          ((dead : σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false) →
              (alloc :
                  ∃ al,
                    σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
                      al.base = c.addr ∧
                        al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) ∧
                          al.ty = some c.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable) →

                          ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),

                              CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c) →
                        motive ⋯) →
            motive t

inductive CerberusHeapLang.Coh :
CerberusHeapLang.Mem → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell → Prop
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Coh.casesOn :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
    {motive : CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m → Sort u} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m) →
        ((cells :
              ∀ (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                Iris.Std.get? m id = some c → CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
            (disj :
                ∀ (id1 id2 : Int) (c1 c2 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                  id1 ≠ id2 →
                    Iris.Std.get? m id1 = some c1 →
                      Iris.Std.get? m id2 = some c2 → CerberusHeapLang.cellsDisjoint c1 c2) →
              motive ⋯) →
          motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Coh.cells :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m →
    ∀ (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
      Iris.Std.get? m id = some c → CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Coh.disj :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m →
    ∀ (id1 id2 : Int) (c1 c2 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
      id1 ≠ id2 →
        Iris.Std.get? m id1 = some c1 →
          Iris.Std.get? m id2 = some c2 → CerberusHeapLang.cellsDisjoint c1 c2
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Coh.mk :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  (∀ (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
      Iris.Std.get? m id = some c → CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
    (∀ (id1 id2 : Int) (c1 c2 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
        id1 ≠ id2 →
          Iris.Std.get? m id1 = some c1 →
            Iris.Std.get? m id2 = some c2 → CerberusHeapLang.cellsDisjoint c1 c2) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.Coh.rec :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
    {motive : CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m → Sort u} →
      ((cells :
            ∀ (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
              Iris.Std.get? m id = some c → CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
          (disj :
              ∀ (id1 id2 : Int) (c1 c2 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                id1 ≠ id2 →
                  Iris.Std.get? m id1 = some c1 →
                    Iris.Std.get? m id2 = some c2 → CerberusHeapLang.cellsDisjoint c1 c2) →
            motive ⋯) →
        (t : CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m) → motive t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Coh.recOn :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
    {motive : CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m → Sort u} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m) →
        ((cells :
              ∀ (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                Iris.Std.get? m id = some c → CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
            (disj :
                ∀ (id1 id2 : Int) (c1 c2 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                  id1 ≠ id2 →
                    Iris.Std.get? m id1 = some c1 →
                      Iris.Std.get? m id2 = some c2 → CerberusHeapLang.cellsDisjoint c1 c2) →
              motive ⋯) →
          motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Coh.store :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (i : Int)
  (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (mv : CerbMem.MemValue),
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m →
    Iris.Std.get? m i = some c →
      CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt c.ty mv →
        CerberusHeapLang.Coh (CerbMem.writeBytesTo σ c.addr (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd)
          (Iris.Std.insert m i
            { addr := c.addr, ty := c.ty, bytes := (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd })

  CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge ds (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge ds' v) =
    CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge (ds ++ ds') v
----

∀ (ds : List dyn_annotation) (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge ds { w := w, ρ := ρ } =
    { w := CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge ds w, ρ := ρ }

CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.mk.inj :
∀ {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {w_1 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal}
  {ρ_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}, { w := w, ρ := ρ } = { w := w_1, ρ := ρ_1 } → w = w_1 ∧ ρ = ρ_1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.mk.injEq :
∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (w_1 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal)
  (ρ_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  ({ w := w, ρ := ρ } = { w := w_1, ρ := ρ_1 }) = (w = w_1 ∧ ρ = ρ_1)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.mk.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} →
    {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} →
      {w' : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} →
        {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} →
          { w := w, ρ := ρ } = { w := w', ρ := ρ' } → (w = w' → ρ = ρ' → P) → P
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.mk.sizeOf_spec :
∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  sizeOf { w := w, ρ := ρ } = 1 + sizeOf w + sizeOf ρ
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {t t' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal} → t = t' → CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.noConfusionType P t t'

inductive CerberusHeapLang.Decomp :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → context → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.annot :
∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false →
    is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false →
      (∀ (n : Nat),
          get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
            List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b)) →
        CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r →
          CerberusHeapLang.Decomp (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) (Cannot [] ds ctx) r
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.apply_eq :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r → apply_ctx ctx r = e
----
inductive CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below :
{motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop} →
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
    {a_1 : context} → {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below.annot :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
  (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
  (hmap :
    ∀ (n : Nat),
      get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
        List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
  (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r),
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a → motive b ctx r a → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below ⋯
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below.casesOn :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
      {a_1 : context} →
        {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
          (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below t → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2} (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below t),
  (∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Redex r), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
        {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r)
        (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 ctx r a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
          {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
          (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
          (hmap :
            ∀ (n : Nat),
              get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
                List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
          (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a)
          (a_ih : motive b ctx r a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        motive_1 t t_1
----

  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}

    {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
      {a_1 : context} →
        {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
          (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below t → Prop},
  (∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Redex r), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
        {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r)
        (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 ctx r a),
        motive_1 a ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
          {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)

          (hmap :
            ∀ (n : Nat),
              get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
                List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
          (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a)
          (a_ih : motive b ctx r a), motive_1 a ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
          {t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2} (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below t),

        motive a a_1 a_2 t

          (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r),
          motive b ctx r a → motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) (Cannot [] ds ctx) r ⋯) →

theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.step_factor :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step (e, ρ, σ) out →
      ∃ r' ρ' σ', CerberusHeapLang.Step (r, ρ, σ) (r', ρ', σ') ∧ out = (apply_ctx ctx r', ρ', σ')

∀ {v : value} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {v_1 : value} {σ_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},

    v = v_1 ∧ σ = σ_1

    (v = v_1 ∧ σ = σ_1)

  {v : value} →
    {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
      {v' : value} →
        {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v' σ' →
            (v = v' → σ = σ' → P) → P

∀ (v : value) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  sizeOf (CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ) = 1 + sizeOf v + sizeOf σ

inductive CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr →
  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome → Prop
----
def CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch.casesOn :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome) → CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (t : CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ a),
  (∀ {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (a : CerberusHeapLang.Step (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ')),
      motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (CerberusHeapLang.envThread e' ρ') σ') ⋯) →
    (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
        (a : e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)),
        motive
          (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
            (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v))
              ρ)
            σ)
          ⋯) →
      (∀ {v : value} (a : e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v) ⋯) →
        (∀ {o : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (a : o.isRefusal)
            (a_1 :
              ∀
                (out :
                  CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                ¬CerberusHeapLang.Step (e, ρ, σ) out)
            (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none), motive o ⋯) →
          motive a t
----

  (∀ {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (a : CerberusHeapLang.Step (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ')),

            motive a t

  {motive_1 :
    {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.FragP a) → CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below t → Prop}

  (∀ (v : value), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
        {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}

      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
            {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
            motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragP e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragP e2)
              (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 a)
              (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below a_1) (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}

                (a_ih : motive b a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
              motive_1 t t_1

def CerberusHeapLang.FragP.casesOn :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragP a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (t : CerberusHeapLang.FragP a),
  (∀ (v : value), motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval v)))) ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
        {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
        (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
        motive
          (generic_expr.Expr []
            (Eaction
              (Paction polarity.Pos
                (Action loc ann
                  (Store0 lk (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty)))
                    (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))) (Pexpr [] () (PEval cv)) mo)))))
          ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
          motive
            (generic_expr.Expr []
              (Eaction
                (Paction polarity.Pos
                  (Action loc ann
                    (Load0 (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty)))
                      (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))) mo)))))
            ⋯) →
        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
            {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
            motive
              (generic_expr.Expr []
                (Eaction
                  (Paction polarity.Pos
                    (Action loc ann
                      (Create (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVinteger align))))
                        (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty))) pref)))))
              ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragP e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragP e2),
              motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2)) ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragP b), motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) ⋯) →
              motive a t
----

∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype}
  {pref : prefix0},
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    CerberusHeapLang.FragP
      (generic_expr.Expr []
        (Eaction
          (Paction polarity.Pos
            (Action loc ann

                (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty))) pref)))))

  (∀ (v : value), motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval v)))) ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}

                  (Store0 lk (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty)))

          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),

            (generic_expr.Expr []
              (Eaction
                (Paction polarity.Pos
                  (Action loc ann
                    (Load0 (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty)))
                      (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))) mo)))))
            ⋯) →

                (Eaction
                  (Paction polarity.Pos
                    (Action loc ann
                      (Create (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVinteger align))))
                        (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty))) pref)))))

              motive e1 a →
                motive e2 a_1 →
                  motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
                    ⋯) →

  CerberusHeapLang.Redex
    (generic_expr.Expr []
      (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
        (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2))

  CerberusHeapLang.Redex

def CerberusHeapLang.Redex.casesOn :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Redex a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (t : CerberusHeapLang.Redex a),
  (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
      {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
      (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
      motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
        {mo : memory_order} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
        motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
          {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) ⋯) →
        (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
            motive
              (generic_expr.Expr []
                (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                  (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2))
              ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
              {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
              motive
                (generic_expr.Expr []
                  (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                    (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2))
                ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (hirr :
                  is_irreducible
                      (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) =
                    false),
                motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b))))
                  ⋯) →
              motive a t
----

{motive : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell → Sort u} →
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) →
    ((addr : Int) →
        (ty : ctype) →
          (bytes : List CerbMem.AbsByte) → motive { addr := addr, ty := ty, bytes := bytes }) →
      motive t

CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell → Nat

ctor CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.mk :
Int → ctype → List CerbMem.AbsByte → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.mk.inj :
∀ {addr : Int} {ty : ctype} {bytes : List CerbMem.AbsByte} {addr_1 : Int} {ty_1 : ctype}
  {bytes_1 : List CerbMem.AbsByte},
  { addr := addr, ty := ty, bytes := bytes } = { addr := addr_1, ty := ty_1, bytes := bytes_1 } →
    addr = addr_1 ∧ ty = ty_1 ∧ bytes = bytes_1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.mk.injEq :
∀ (addr : Int) (ty : ctype) (bytes : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (addr_1 : Int) (ty_1 : ctype)
  (bytes_1 : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  ({ addr := addr, ty := ty, bytes := bytes } = { addr := addr_1, ty := ty_1, bytes := bytes_1 }) =
    (addr = addr_1 ∧ ty = ty_1 ∧ bytes = bytes_1)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.mk.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {addr : Int} →
    {ty : ctype} →
      {bytes : List CerbMem.AbsByte} →
        {addr' : Int} →
          {ty' : ctype} →
            {bytes' : List CerbMem.AbsByte} →
              { addr := addr, ty := ty, bytes := bytes } =
                  { addr := addr', ty := ty', bytes := bytes' } →
                (addr = addr' → ty = ty' → bytes = bytes' → P) → P
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.mk.sizeOf_spec :
∀ (addr : Int) (ty : ctype) (bytes : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  sizeOf { addr := addr, ty := ty, bytes := bytes } = 1 + sizeOf addr + sizeOf ty + sizeOf bytes
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {t t' : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} → t = t' → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.noConfusionType P t t'
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.noConfusionType :
Sort u → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell → Sort u
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.rec :
{motive : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell → Sort u} →
  ((addr : Int) →
      (ty : ctype) →
        (bytes : List CerbMem.AbsByte) → motive { addr := addr, ty := ty, bytes := bytes }) →
    (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) → motive t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell.recOn :
{motive : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell → Sort u} →
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) →
    ((addr : Int) →
        (ty : ctype) →
          (bytes : List CerbMem.AbsByte) → motive { addr := addr, ty := ty, bytes := bytes }) →
      motive t

ctor CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS.mk :
{hlc : outParam Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [invGS : Iris.InvGS_gen hlc GF] →
      Iris.genHeapGS Int CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF →
        CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS.mk.noConfusion :
{hlc : outParam Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    {P : Sort u} →
      {invGS : Iris.InvGS_gen hlc GF} →
        {heap : Iris.genHeapGS Int CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF} →
          {invGS' : Iris.InvGS_gen hlc GF} →
            {heap' : Iris.genHeapGS Int CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF} →
              { invGS := invGS, heap := heap } = { invGS := invGS', heap := heap' } →
                (invGS ≍ invGS' → heap ≍ heap' → P) → P
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
    {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
      {t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF} →
        {hlc' : Iris.HasLC} →
          {GF' : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
            {t' : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc' GF'} →
              hlc = hlc' → GF = GF' → t ≍ t' → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS.noConfusionType P t t'
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS.noConfusionType :
Sort u →
  {hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
    {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
      CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF →
        {hlc' : Iris.HasLC} →
          {GF' : Iris.BundledGFunctors} → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc' GF' → Sort u
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS.rec :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    {motive : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF → Sort u} →
      ([invGS : Iris.InvGS_gen hlc GF] →
          (heap : Iris.genHeapGS Int CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF) →
            motive { invGS := invGS, heap := heap }) →
        (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF) → motive t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS.recOn :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    {motive : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF → Sort u} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF) →

            (heap : Iris.genHeapGS Int CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell GF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF) →
              motive { invGS := invGS, heap := heap }) →
          motive t

                { toInvGpreS := toInvGpreS', heap_pre := heap_pre' } →

def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot.elim :
{motive : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → Sort u} →
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) →
    t.ctorIdx = 1 →
      ((ds : List dyn_annotation) → (v : value) → motive (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) →
        motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot.inj :
∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value} {ds_1 : List dyn_annotation} {v_1 : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v = CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds_1 v_1 →
    ds = ds_1 ∧ v = v_1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot.injEq :
∀ (ds : List dyn_annotation) (v : value) (ds_1 : List dyn_annotation) (v_1 : value),
  (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v = CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds_1 v_1) =
    (ds = ds_1 ∧ v = v_1)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} →
    {v : value} →
      {ds' : List dyn_annotation} →
        {v' : value} →
          CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v = CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds' v' →
            (ds = ds' → v = v' → P) → P
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot.sizeOf_spec :
∀ (ds : List dyn_annotation) (v : value),
  sizeOf (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v) = 1 + sizeOf ds + sizeOf v
----

{motive : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → Sort u} →
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) →
    ((v : value) → motive (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) →
      ((ds : List dyn_annotation) → (v : value) → motive (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) →
        motive t

    (t : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) →

∀ (ds ds' : List dyn_annotation) (v : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal),
  CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge ds (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge ds' v) =
    CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge (ds ++ ds') v

  (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v = CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v_1) = (v = v_1)

    CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v = CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v' → (v = v' → P) → P

inductive CerberusHeapLang.Step :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem →
  CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.annot_ctx :
∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b b' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ') →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b), ρ, σ)
        (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b'), ρ', σ')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.annot_inv :
∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b), ρ, σ) out →
    (CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false ∧
        ∃ b' ρ' σ',
          CerberusHeapLang.Step (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ') ∧
            out = (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b'), ρ', σ')) ∨
      ∃ a2 ds2 c,
        b = generic_expr.Expr a2 (Eannot ds2 c) ∧
          out = (generic_expr.Expr (a ++ a2) (Eannot (ds ++ ds2) c), ρ, σ)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.annot_merge :
∀ {a1 a2 : List annot} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step
    (generic_expr.Expr a1 (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr a2 (Eannot ds2 b))), ρ, σ)
    (generic_expr.Expr (a1 ++ a2) (Eannot (ds1 ++ ds2) b), ρ, σ)
----
inductive CerberusHeapLang.Step.below :
{motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1 → Prop} →
  {a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1 → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.annot_ctx :
∀
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1 → Prop}
  {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b b' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
  (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.Step (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ')),
  CerberusHeapLang.Step.below a_2 →
    motive (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ') a_2 → CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.annot_merge :
∀
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1 → Prop}
  {a1 a2 : List annot} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.casesOn :
∀
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1 → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1) → CerberusHeapLang.Step.below t → Prop}
  {a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {t : CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1} (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Step.below t),
  (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool}
      {pe1 pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
      {mo : memory_order} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
      {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty))
      (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv))) (h3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = some cv)
      (hmv : memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv)
      (hmem : CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.storeM loc ty lk pv mv) σ = some (fp, σ')),
      motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
        {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
        {mo : memory_order} {mval : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
        {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
        (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty))
        (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))
        (hmem : CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc ty pv) σ = some ((fp, mval), σ')),
        motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
          {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype}
          {pref : prefix0} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
          {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vobject (OVinteger align)))
          (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vctype ty))
          (hmem :
            CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty none none) σ =
              some (pv, σ')),
          motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
            motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
              {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
              {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
            (∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern} {e1 e1' e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.Step (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ'))
                (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Step.below a_2) (a_ih : motive (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ') a_2),
                motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
              (∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b b' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                  {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                  (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
                  (a_3 : CerberusHeapLang.Step (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ'))
                  (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Step.below a_3) (a_ih : motive (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ') a_3),
                  motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                (∀ {a1 a2 : List annot} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation}
                    {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                    {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                  motive_1 t t_1
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.create :

  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1 → Prop}
  {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
  {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0}

rec CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.rec :
∀
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1 → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1) → CerberusHeapLang.Step.below t → Prop},
  (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool}
      {pe1 pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
      {mo : memory_order} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
      {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty))
      (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv))) (h3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = some cv)
      (hmv : memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv)
      (hmem : CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.storeM loc ty lk pv mv) σ = some (fp, σ')),
      motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
        {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
        {mo : memory_order} {mval : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
        {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
        (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty))
        (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))
        (hmem : CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc ty pv) σ = some ((fp, mval), σ')),
        motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
          {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype}
          {pref : prefix0} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
          {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vobject (OVinteger align)))
          (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vctype ty))
          (hmem :
            CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty none none) σ =
              some (pv, σ')),
          motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
            motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
              {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
              {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
            (∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern} {e1 e1' e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Step (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ'))
                (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Step.below a_1) (a_ih : motive (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ') a_1),
                motive_1 a_1 ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
              (∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b b' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                  {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                  (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
                  (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.Step (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ'))
                  (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Step.below a_2) (a_ih : motive (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ') a_2),
                  motive_1 a_2 ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                (∀ {a1 a2 : List annot} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation}
                    {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                    {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                  ∀
                    {a a_1 :
                      CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                    {t : CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1} (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Step.below t),
                    motive_1 t t_1
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.sseq_annot :
∀
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1 → Prop}
  {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
  {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}, CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.sseq_ctx :
∀
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1 → Prop}
  {a : List annot} {pat : pattern} {e1 e1' e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Step (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ')),
  CerberusHeapLang.Step.below a_1 →
    motive (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ') a_1 → CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯

ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.sseq_pure :

  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1 → Prop}
  {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.below.store :
∀
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1 → Prop}
  {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool}
  {pe1 pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
  {mo : memory_order} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty))
  (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv))) (h3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = some cv)
  (hmv : memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv)
  (hmem : CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.storeM loc ty lk pv mv) σ = some (fp, σ')),
  CerberusHeapLang.Step.below ⋯

theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.brecOn :
∀
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1 → Prop}
  {a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1),
  (∀ (a a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_2), CerberusHeapLang.Step.below t → motive a a_2 t) →
    motive a a_1 t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Step.casesOn :
∀
  {motive :
    (a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1 → Prop}
  {a a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.Step a a_1),
  (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool}
      {pe1 pe2 pe3 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
      {mo : memory_order} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
      {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty))
      (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv))) (h3 : valueFromPexpr pe3 = some cv)
      (hmv : memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv)
      (hmem : CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.storeM loc ty lk pv mv) σ = some (fp, σ')),
      motive
        (generic_expr.Expr a
            (Eaction (Paction polarity.Pos (Action loc ann (Store0 lk pe1 pe2 pe3 mo)))),
          ρ, σ)
        (generic_expr.Expr []
            (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp] (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval Vunit))))),
          ρ, σ')
        ⋯) →
    (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
        {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
        {mo : memory_order} {mval : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
        {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
        (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty))
        (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))
        (hmem : CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc ty pv) σ = some ((fp, mval), σ')),
        motive
          (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction (Paction polarity.Pos (Action loc ann (Load0 pe1 pe2 mo)))),
            ρ, σ)
          (generic_expr.Expr []
              (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (valueFromMemValue mval).snd))))),
            ρ, σ')
          ⋯) →
      (∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
          {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype}
          {pref : prefix0} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
          {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} (h1 : valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vobject (OVinteger align)))
          (h2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vctype ty))
          (hmem :
            CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty none none) σ =
              some (pv, σ')),
          motive
            (generic_expr.Expr a
                (Eaction (Paction polarity.Pos (Action loc ann (Create pe1 pe2 pref)))),
              ρ, σ)
            (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv))))), ρ, σ')
            ⋯) →
        (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
            motive
              (generic_expr.Expr a
                  (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                    (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2),
                ev0 :: evs, σ)
              (e2, ev0 :: evs, σ) ⋯) →
          (∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
              {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
              {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
              motive
                (generic_expr.Expr a
                    (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                      (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2),
                  ev0 :: evs, σ)
                (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e2), ev0 :: evs, σ) ⋯) →
            (∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern} {e1 e1' e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.Step (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ')),
                motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq pat e1 e2), ρ, σ)
                  (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq pat e1' e2), ρ', σ') ⋯) →
              (∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b b' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                  {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
                  (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
                  (a_3 : CerberusHeapLang.Step (b, ρ, σ) (b', ρ', σ')),
                  motive (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b), ρ, σ)
                    (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b'), ρ', σ') ⋯) →
                (∀ {a1 a2 : List annot} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation}
                    {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
                    {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
                    motive
                      (generic_expr.Expr a1 (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr a2 (Eannot ds2 b))), ρ,
                        σ)
                      (generic_expr.Expr (a1 ++ a2) (Eannot (ds1 ++ ds2) b), ρ, σ) ⋯) →
                  motive a a_1 t
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.create :
∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
  {pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vobject (OVinteger align)) →
    valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vctype ty) →
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty none none) σ =
          some (pv, σ') →
        CerberusHeapLang.Step
          (generic_expr.Expr a
              (Eaction (Paction polarity.Pos (Action loc ann (Create pe1 pe2 pref)))),
            ρ, σ)
          (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv))))), ρ, σ')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.create_canonical :
∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
  {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty none none) σ = some (pv, σ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step
      (generic_expr.Expr a
          (Eaction
            (Paction polarity.Pos
              (Action loc ann
                (Create (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVinteger align))))
                  (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty))) pref)))),
        ρ, σ)
      (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv))))), ρ, σ')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.create_inv :
∀ {a : List annot} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation}
  {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step
      (generic_expr.Expr a
          (Eaction
            (Paction polarity.Pos
              (Action loc ann
                (Create (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVinteger align))))
                  (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vctype ty))) pref)))),
        ρ, σ)
      out →
    ∃ pv σ',
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty none none) σ =
          some (pv, σ') ∧
        out = (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv))))), ρ, σ')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.env_invariant :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ') → ρ' = ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.env_invariant' :
∀ {c c' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step c c' → c'.snd.fst = c.snd.fst
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Step.esize_succ :
∀ {c c' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Step c c' → CerberusHeapLang.esize c'.fst ≤ CerberusHeapLang.esize c.fst + 1

ctor CerberusHeapLang.Step.load :

  {mval : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  valueFromPexpr pe1 = some (Vctype ty) →
    valueFromPexpr pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)) →
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc ty pv) σ = some ((fp, mval), σ') →
        CerberusHeapLang.Step
          (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction (Paction polarity.Pos (Action loc ann (Load0 pe1 pe2 mo)))),
            ρ, σ)
          (generic_expr.Expr []
              (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (valueFromMemValue mval).snd))))),
            ρ, σ')

  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order} {mval : CerbMem.MemValue} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},

    CerberusHeapLang.Step
