def CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomes :
Nat →
  CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr →
    CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → List CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome
----
def CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → List core_step2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_beta_annot :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr []
        (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
          (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2)) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e (ev0 :: evs) σ =
          [Step_tau2 "Esseq Eannot" TSK_Misc
              (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e2)))
                (ev0 :: evs))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_beta_pure :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
  {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr []
        (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
          (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2)) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e (ev0 :: evs) σ =
          [Step_tau2 "Esseq" TSK_Misc (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (apply_ctx ctx e2) (ev0 :: evs))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_create :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
            [Step_action_request2 "CreateRequest" loc 0 (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                (stExceptUndef_return
                  (CreateRequest2 pref align ty (get_with_address []) none fun x pv =>
                    CerberusHeapLang.envThread
                      (apply_ctx ctx
                        (generic_expr.Expr []
                          (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))))))
                      ρ))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_done :
∀ (v : value) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) ρ σ =
    [Step_done2 v]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_load :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
            [Step_action_request2 "LoadRequest" loc 0 (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                (stExceptUndef_return
                  (LoadRequest2 mo ty pv fun x fp mval =>
                    CerberusHeapLang.envThread
                      (apply_ctx ctx
                        (generic_expr.Expr []
                          (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                            (generic_expr.Expr []
                              (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (valueFromMemValue mval).snd)))))))
                      ρ))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_merge :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation}
  {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) →
    is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) =
        false →
      CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
        ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
            [Step_tau2 "Eannot" TSK_Misc
                (CerberusHeapLang.envThread
                  (apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot (ds1 ++ ds2) b))) ρ)]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_remove_annot :
∀ (ds : List dyn_annotation) (v : value) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) ρ σ =
    [Step_tau2 "CTX, Eannot(value)" TSK_Misc
        (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) ρ)]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_store :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
  {mo : memory_order} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv →
          ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
            CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
              [Step_action_request2 "StoreRequest" loc 0 (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                  (stExceptUndef_return
                    (StoreRequest2 mo ty lk pv mv fun x fp =>
                      CerberusHeapLang.envThread
                        (apply_ctx ctx
                          (generic_expr.Expr []
                            (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                              (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval Vunit)))))))
                        ρ))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_store_illtyped :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
  {mo : memory_order},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = none →
        ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
            [Step_error2
                ((CerbLocation.stringFromLocation loc).append
                  ("the value of a store(".append
                    ((CerbPP.stringFromCore_ctype (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty))).append
                      (") didn't match the lvalue type: ".append
                        (CerbPP.stringFromCore_value cv)))))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engine_complete :
∀ (aid : Nat) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value))
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP e →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∃ o,
        CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomes aid e (ev0 :: evs) σ = [o] ∧
          CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e (ev0 :: evs) σ o
----

  (CerberusHeapLang.envThread e ρ).arena = e

def CerberusHeapLang.esize :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → Nat
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize.eq_def :
∀ (x : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.esize x =
    match x with
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq a_1 e1 e2) =>
      1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.esize e1) (CerberusHeapLang.esize e2)
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot a_1 b) => 1 + CerberusHeapLang.esize b
    | x => 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_action :
∀ {a : List annot} {p : generic_paction core_run_annotation Unit sym},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction p)) = 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_annot :
∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b)) = 1 + CerberusHeapLang.esize b
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_ofVal_annot :
∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) = 2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_ofVal_pure :
∀ {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) = 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_pos :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr), 1 ≤ CerberusHeapLang.esize e
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_pure :
∀ {a : List annot} {pe : pexpr}, CerberusHeapLang.esize (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure pe)) = 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_sseq :
∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq pat e1 e2)) =
    1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.esize e1) (CerberusHeapLang.esize e2)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibit :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (x y : CerbMem.PointerValue) (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation)
  (mo : memory_order) (bs bs' : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ty' : ctype),
  CerberusHeapLang.triple
    iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bs ∗ y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty' ; bs')
    (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy x CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal mo)
    fun x_1 =>
    iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes ∗
        y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty' ; bs')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitA_engine :
∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
  n ≤ 999998 →
    (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
        CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progA)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
      CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progA)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
        ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progA)
                CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ =
              CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
            v = CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitA_prod :
∀ (fs : CerbFS.FsState) (args : List String),
  ∃ dres dst',
    CerbND.runND (drive fmapEmpty false (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile CerberusHeapLang.progAProd) args)
          (initial_driver_state (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile CerberusHeapLang.progAProd) fs) =
        [(Active dres, [], dst')] ∧
      dres.dres_core_value = CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal ∧
        CerbMem.readBytesFrom dst'.layout_state CerberusHeapLang.pxAddr 4 =
          (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval).snd
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitA_semantic :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple CerberusHeapLang.progA CerberusHeapLang.mA fun v Q =>
    v = CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal ∧ Q = CerberusHeapLang.mA7
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitA_terminates :
∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids 6 (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progA)
      CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ =
    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal CerberusHeapLang.σ₁
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitB_engine :
∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
  n ≤ 999999 →
    (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
        CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progB)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
      CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progB)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
        ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progB)
                CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ =
              CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
            v = Vunit ∧
              CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ' CerberusHeapLang.xAddr 4 =
                  (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval).snd ∧
                CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ' CerberusHeapLang.yAddr 4 = CerberusHeapLang.bytesY
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitB_semantic :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple CerberusHeapLang.progB
    (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union CerberusHeapLang.mA CerberusHeapLang.mF) fun v Q =>
    ∃ Q₀,
      (v = Vunit ∧ Q₀ = CerberusHeapLang.mA7) ∧
        Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint Q₀ CerberusHeapLang.mF ∧
          Q = Iris.Std.PartialMap.union Q₀ CerberusHeapLang.mF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitC_engine :
∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
  n ≤ 999998 →
    (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
        CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progC)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
      CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progC)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
        ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progC)
                CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ =
              CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
            CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ' CerberusHeapLang.xAddr 4 = CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes ∧
              CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ' CerberusHeapLang.yAddr 4 = CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitC_semantic :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple CerberusHeapLang.progC CerberusHeapLang.mB fun x Q =>
    Q = CerberusHeapLang.mC
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitC_triple :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (x y : CerbMem.PointerValue) (loc loc' : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ann' : core_run_annotation)
  (mo mo' : memory_order) (bty : core_base_type) (bsx bsy : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerberusHeapLang.triple
    iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bsx ∗
        y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bsy)
    (CerberusHeapLang.sseqExpr bty
      (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy x CerberusHeapLang.fiveVal mo)
      (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc' ann' CerberusHeapLang.intTy y CerberusHeapLang.sixVal mo'))
    fun x_1 =>
    iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes ∗
        y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.finalize_done :
∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (s : String) (dst : driver_state)
  (th : thread_state) (v : value),
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    th.arena = mk_value_e v →
      finalize tds s dst =
        { dres_blocked := dst.blocked, dres_concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state,
          dres_driver_steps := dst.dr_step_counter, dres_core_value := v,
          dres_stdout := List.foldr String.append "" (toList dst.core_state0.io.stdout),
          dres_stderr := List.foldr String.append "" (toList dst.core_state0.io.stderr) }

theorem CerberusHeapLang.loadM_success :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (loc : CerbLocation.Loc),
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    CerberusHeapLang.cellLoadTrap c = false →
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc c.ty (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id c.addr)) σ =
        some
          ((CerbMem.Footprint.FP CerbMem.FootprintAccess.R c.addr ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty),
              CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c),
            σ)

        CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell
          { addr := CerberusHeapLang.xAddr, ty := CerberusHeapLang.intTy,

      CerberusHeapLang.σ₁)

CerbMem.PointerValue → ctype → List CerbMem.AbsByte → value

theorem CerberusHeapLang.loaded_seven :
CerberusHeapLang.loadedVal CerberusHeapLang.xPtr CerberusHeapLang.intTy
    (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval).snd =
  CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loaded_seven_at :
∀ (i a : Int),
  CerberusHeapLang.loadedVal (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr i a) CerberusHeapLang.intTy
      (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval).snd =
    CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal

theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_step_action :
∀ (fl : Nat) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)) {dst dst' : driver_state} {th : thread_state}
  {s : String} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {req : action_request2 thread_state},
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    step_ctx tds dst.layout_state dst.core_file dst.core_extern 0 (none, th) =
        [Step_action_request2 s loc 0 false (stExceptUndef_return req)] →
      CerberusHeapLang.runOne (action_request_sequential2 loc 0 dst.core_run_state0.aid_supply req)
            { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
              core_state0 := dst.core_state0,
              core_run_state0 :=
                have __src := dst.core_run_state0;
                { tid_supply := __src.tid_supply, aid_supply := dst.core_run_state0.aid_supply + 1,
                  excluded_supply := __src.excluded_supply, sym_supply := __src.sym_supply,
                  labeled := __src.labeled },
              layout_state := dst.layout_state, concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state,
              fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0, trace := dst.trace, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc,
              blocked := dst.blocked, dr_step_counter := dst.dr_step_counter } =
          (NDactive (), dst') →
        CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl.succ tds acc [0]) dst =
          CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl tds acc [0]) dst'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_step_done :
∀ (fl : Nat) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)) {dst : driver_state} {th : thread_state} {v : value},
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    step_ctx tds dst.layout_state dst.core_file dst.core_extern 0 (none, th) = [Step_done2 v] →
      CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl.succ.succ tds acc [0]) dst =
        (NDactive (fmapAddBy defaultCompare 0 [Step_done2 v] acc), dst)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_step_tau :
∀ (fl : Nat) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)) {dst : driver_state} {th th' : thread_state}
  {s : String},
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    step_ctx tds dst.layout_state dst.core_file dst.core_extern 0 (none, th) =
        [Step_tau2 s TSK_Misc th'] →
      CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl.succ tds acc [0]) dst =
        CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl tds acc [0])
          (have __src :=
            { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
              core_state0 := dst.core_state0, core_run_state0 := dst.core_run_state0,
              layout_state := dst.layout_state, concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state,
              fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0, trace := dst.trace, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc,
              blocked := dst.blocked, dr_step_counter := dst.dr_step_counter + 1 };
          { core_file := __src.core_file, core_extern := __src.core_extern,
            core_state0 := update_thread_state 0 th' dst.core_state0,
            core_run_state0 := __src.core_run_state0, layout_state := __src.layout_state,
            concurrency_state := __src.concurrency_state, fs_state0 := __src.fs_state0,
            trace := __src.trace, symbolic_assoc := __src.symbolic_assoc, blocked := __src.blocked,
            dr_step_counter := __src.dr_step_counter })
----

def CerberusHeapLang.pointsToCell :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerbMem.PointerValue → Iris.DFrac → ctype → List CerbMem.AbsByte → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pointsToCell_iff :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue) (dq : Iris.DFrac) (ty : ctype) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs ⊣⊢
    ∃ i a, ⌜pv = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr i a⌝ ∗ i ↦{dq} { addr := a, ty := ty, bytes := bs }

theorem CerberusHeapLang.prepare_exit_single :
∀ (cs : core_state) (th : thread_state) (v : value),
  cs.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    prepare_exit cs v =
      {
        thread_states :=
          [(0, none,
              { arena := mk_value_e v, stack0 := Stack_empty, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                current_loc := th.current_loc })],
        io := cs.io }

theorem CerberusHeapLang.primStep_eq :
∀ (r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (obs : List Empty)
  (r' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (efs : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt),
  Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.primStep (r, σ) obs (r', σ', efs) ↔
    CerberusHeapLang.Step (r.e, r.ρ, σ) (r'.e, r'.ρ, σ') ∧ efs = []

theorem CerberusHeapLang.process_done :
∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (cont : Bool → ndM Unit step_kind driver_error (mem_constraint CerbMem.IntegerValue) driver_state)
  (v : value) (dst : driver_state) (th : thread_state),
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    CerberusHeapLang.runOne (process_core_step2 tds false cont (Step_done2 v)) dst =
      (NDactive (),
        { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
          core_state0 :=
            have __src := dst.core_state0;
            {
              thread_states :=
                [(0, none,
                    { arena := mk_value_e v, stack0 := Stack_empty, errno := th.errno,
                      env := th.env, current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt,
                      exec_loc := th.exec_loc, current_loc := th.current_loc })],
              io := __src.io },
          core_run_state0 := dst.core_run_state0, layout_state := dst.layout_state,
          concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state, fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0,
          trace := dst.trace, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc, blocked := dst.blocked,
          dr_step_counter := dst.dr_step_counter })

theorem CerberusHeapLang.prodA_pre :
∀ (aids : Nat → Nat) (n : Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (2 + n) (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progAProd)
      CerberusHeapLang.prodMem₀ =
    CerberusHeapLang.drive (fun i => aids (i + 2)) n
      (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progAProdC) CerberusHeapLang.σcP
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.prodA_terminates :
∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids 6 (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progAProdC)
      CerberusHeapLang.σcP =
    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal CerberusHeapLang.σ1P

theorem CerberusHeapLang.prod_loop_done :
∀ (th₀ : thread_state),
  th₀.stack0 = Stack_empty →
    ∀ {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)},
      th₀.env = ev0 :: evs →
        ∀ (v : value) (σfin : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (n fl : Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
          (dst : driver_state) (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)),
          CerberusHeapLang.FragP e →
            dst.core_state0.thread_states =
                [(0, none,
                    { arena := e, stack0 := th₀.stack0, errno := th₀.errno, env := th₀.env,
                      current_proc_opt := th₀.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th₀.exec_loc,
                      current_loc := th₀.current_loc })] →
              CerberusHeapLang.esize e + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                n + 2 ≤ fl →
                  (∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
                      CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e)
                          dst.layout_state =
                        CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σfin) →
                    ∃ rs' tr ctr,
                      CerberusHeapLang.runOne
                          (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl fmapEmpty acc [0]) dst =
                        (NDactive (fmapAddBy defaultCompare 0 [Step_done2 v] acc),
                          { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
                            core_state0 :=
                              have __src := dst.core_state0;
                              {
                                thread_states :=
                                  [(0, none,
                                      {
                                        arena :=
                                          CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v),
                                        stack0 := th₀.stack0, errno := th₀.errno, env := th₀.env,
                                        current_proc_opt := th₀.current_proc_opt,
                                        exec_loc := th₀.exec_loc,
                                        current_loc := th₀.current_loc })],
                                io := __src.io },
                            core_run_state0 := rs', layout_state := σfin,
                            concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state, fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0,
                            trace := tr, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc,
                            blocked := dst.blocked, dr_step_counter := ctr })
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.prod_run_eq :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP e →
    ∀ (v : value) (σfin : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (k : Nat),
      (∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
          CerberusHeapLang.drive aids k (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e) CerberusHeapLang.prodMem₀ =
            CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σfin) →
        CerberusHeapLang.esize e + k + 2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
          ∀ (fs : CerbFS.FsState) (args : List String),
            ∃ dres dst',
              CerbND.runND (drive fmapEmpty false (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) args)
                    (initial_driver_state (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) fs) =
                  [(Active dres, [], dst')] ∧
                dres.dres_core_value = v ∧
                  dres.dres_blocked = false ∧
                    dres.dres_stdout = "" ∧ dres.dres_stderr = "" ∧ dst'.layout_state = σfin

theorem CerberusHeapLang.readBytesFrom_writeBytesTo_disjoint :
∀ (st : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (a' : Int) (n : Nat),
  a' + ↑n ≤ a ∨ a + ↑bs.length ≤ a' →

theorem CerberusHeapLang.semAProd :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple CerberusHeapLang.progAProdC CerberusHeapLang.mAP fun v Q =>
    v = CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal ∧ Q = CerberusHeapLang.mAP7
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.sem_triple_prod :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP e →
    ∀ (ec : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (P : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap)
      (post : value → CerberusHeapLang.CellMap → Prop),
      CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple ec P post →
        ∀ (R : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap),
          Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint P R →
            ∀ (σc : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (j : Nat),
              (∀ (aids : Nat → Nat) (n : Nat),
                  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (j + n) (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e)
                      CerberusHeapLang.prodMem₀ =
                    CerberusHeapLang.drive (fun i => aids (i + j)) n
                      (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread ec) σc) →
                CerberusHeapLang.Sat σc (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union P R) →
                  ∀ (v : value) (σfin : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (k : Nat),
                    (∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
                        CerberusHeapLang.drive aids k (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread ec) σc =
                          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σfin) →
                      CerberusHeapLang.esize e + (j + k) + 2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                        CerberusHeapLang.esize ec + k ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                          ∀ (fs : CerbFS.FsState) (args : List String),
                            ∃ dres dst',
                              CerbND.runND
                                    (drive fmapEmpty false (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) args)
                                    (initial_driver_state (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) fs) =
                                  [(Active dres, [], dst')] ∧
                                dres.dres_core_value = v ∧
                                  dst'.layout_state = σfin ∧
                                    ∃ Q,
                                      post v Q ∧
                                        Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint Q R ∧
                                          CerberusHeapLang.Sat σfin (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union Q R)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.semantic_frame :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF]
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP e →
    ∀ {P : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap} (F : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap)
      {post : value → CerberusHeapLang.CellMap → Prop},
      Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint P F →
        CerberusHeapLang.ProvenTriple GF e P post →
          CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple e (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union P F) fun v Q =>
            ∃ Q₀, post v Q₀ ∧ Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint Q₀ F ∧ Q = Iris.Std.PartialMap.union Q₀ F
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.semantic_triple_sound :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF]
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP e →
    ∀ {P : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap} {post : value → CerberusHeapLang.CellMap → Prop},
      CerberusHeapLang.ProvenTriple GF e P post → CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple e P post
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.spike_engine_adequacy :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (e₀ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
  (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP e₀ →
    CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀ m₀ →
      ∀ (ψ : value → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop),
        (∀ [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
            ([∗map] i ↦ c ∈ m₀, i ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} c) ⊢
              WP { e := e₀, ρ := CerberusHeapLang.spikeEnv } @ Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
                ⊤ {{ w, ∀ σ' ns κs nt, Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗ ⌜ψ w.val σ'⌝ }} ) →
          ∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
            CerberusHeapLang.esize e₀ + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
                  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e₀) σ₀ ≠
                    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
                CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e₀) σ₀ ≠
                    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
                  ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e₀) σ₀ =
                        CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                      ψ v σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.spike_step_adequacy :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt)
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m₀ →
    ∀ (φp : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop),
      (∀ [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
          ([∗map] i ↦ c ∈ m₀, i ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} c) ⊢
            WP e @ Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
              ⊤ {{ v, ∀ σ' ns κs nt, Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗ ⌜φp v σ'⌝ }} ) →
        ∀ {t2 : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt} {σ2 : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
          FromMathlib.Relation.ReflTransGen Iris.ProgramLogic.Language.ErasedStep ([e], σ)
              (t2, σ2) →
            (∀ (e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt),
                e2 ∈ t2 → Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.NotStuck (e2, σ2)) ∧
              ∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal) (t2' : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt),
                t2 = CerberusHeapLang.ofValRt w :: t2' → φp w σ2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.spike_wp_wand :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt}
  {Φ Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  WP e @ s ; E {{ Φ }} ⊢ (∀ v, Φ v -∗ Ψ v) -∗ WP e @ s ; E {{ Ψ }}
----
def CerberusHeapLang.sseqExpr :
core_base_type → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.sseq_annot_det :
∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
  {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {obs : List Empty} {r' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt}
  {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {eₜ : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt},
  Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.primStep
      ({
          e :=
            generic_expr.Expr a
              (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2),
          ρ := ev0 :: evs },
        σ)
      obs (r', σ', eₜ) →
    obs = [] ∧ σ' = σ ∧ r' = { e := generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e2), ρ := ev0 :: evs } ∧ eₜ = []
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.sseq_pure_det :
∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {obs : List Empty}
  {r' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {eₜ : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt},
  Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.primStep
      ({
          e :=
            generic_expr.Expr a
              (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2),
          ρ := ev0 :: evs },
        σ)
      obs (r', σ', eₜ) →
    obs = [] ∧ σ' = σ ∧ r' = { e := e2, ρ := ev0 :: evs } ∧ eₜ = []
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stateInterp_eq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (ns : Nat) (κs : List Empty) (nt : Nat),
  Iris.stateInterp σ ns κs nt = iprop(∃ m, ⌜CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m⌝ ∗ Iris.genHeapInterp m)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stateInterp_iff :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (ns : Nat) (κs : List Empty) (nt : Nat),
  Iris.stateInterp σ ns κs nt ⊣⊢ ∃ m, ⌜CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m⌝ ∗ Iris.genHeapInterp m
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_beta_annot :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr []
        (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
          (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2)) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
        (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
        (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
        th.arena = e →
          ∀ {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)},
            th.env = ev0 :: evs →
              step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
                [Step_tau2 "Esseq Eannot" TSK_Misc
                    { arena := apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e2)),
                      stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                      current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                      current_loc := th.current_loc }]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_beta_pure :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
  {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr []
        (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
          (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2)) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
        (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
        (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
        th.arena = e →
          ∀ {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)},
            th.env = ev0 :: evs →
              step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
                [Step_tau2 "Esseq" TSK_Misc
                    { arena := apply_ctx ctx e2, stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno,
                      env := th.env, current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt,
                      exec_loc := th.exec_loc, current_loc := th.current_loc }]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_create :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
              [Step_action_request2 "CreateRequest" loc tid (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                  (stExceptUndef_return
                    (CreateRequest2 pref align ty (get_with_address []) none fun x pv =>
                      {
                        arena :=
                          apply_ctx ctx
                            (generic_expr.Expr []
                              (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))))),
                        stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                        current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                        current_loc := th.current_loc }))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_done :
∀ (v : value) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
  (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
  (th : thread_state),
  th.arena = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v) →
    th.stack0 = Stack_empty → step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (none, th) = [Step_done2 v]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_load :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
              [Step_action_request2 "LoadRequest" loc tid (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                  (stExceptUndef_return
                    (LoadRequest2 mo ty pv fun x fp mval =>
                      {
                        arena :=
                          apply_ctx ctx
                            (generic_expr.Expr []
                              (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                                (generic_expr.Expr []
                                  (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (valueFromMemValue mval).snd)))))),
                        stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                        current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                        current_loc := th.current_loc }))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_merge :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation}
  {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) →
    is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) =
        false →
      CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
              [Step_tau2 "Eannot" TSK_Misc
                  { arena := apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot (ds1 ++ ds2) b)),
                    stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                    current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                    current_loc := th.current_loc }]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_remove_annot :
∀ (ds : List dyn_annotation) (v : value) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym)
  (tid : Nat) (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
  th.arena = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v) →
    step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
      [Step_tau2 "CTX, Eannot(value)" TSK_Misc
          { arena := CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v), stack0 := th.stack0,
            errno := th.errno, env := th.env, current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt,
            exec_loc := th.exec_loc, current_loc := th.current_loc }]

theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_store :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
  {mo : memory_order} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)),
          memValueFromValue tds (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv →
            ∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation)
              (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat) (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
              th.arena = e →
                step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
                  [Step_action_request2 "StoreRequest" loc tid (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                      (stExceptUndef_return
                        (StoreRequest2 mo ty lk pv mv fun x fp =>
                          {
                            arena :=
                              apply_ctx ctx
                                (generic_expr.Expr []
                                  (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                                    (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval Vunit)))))),
                            stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                            current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                            current_loc := th.current_loc }))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_store_illtyped :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
  {mo : memory_order},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)),
        memValueFromValue tds (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = none →
          ∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation)
            (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat) (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
            th.arena = e →
              step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
                [Step_error2
                    ((CerbLocation.stringFromLocation loc).append
                      ("the value of a store(".append
                        ((CerbPP.stringFromCore_ctype (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty))).append
                          (") didn't match the lvalue type: ".append
                            (CerbPP.stringFromCore_value cv)))))]

theorem CerberusHeapLang.storeM_success :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (mv : CerbMem.MemValue)
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc),
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt c.ty mv →
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
          (CerbMem.storeM loc c.ty false (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id c.addr) mv) σ =
        some
          (CerbMem.Footprint.FP CerbMem.FootprintAccess.W c.addr ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty),
            CerbMem.writeBytesTo σ c.addr (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd)

    (CerbMem.storeM CerberusHeapLang.loc0 CerberusHeapLang.intTy false CerberusHeapLang.xPtr

  some (CerberusHeapLang.fpS, CerberusHeapLang.σ₁)

    (CerbMem.storeM CerberusHeapLang.loc0 CerberusHeapLang.intTy false CerberusHeapLang.pxPtr
      CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval)

  CerberusHeapLang.toValRt { e := e, ρ := ρ } =

  CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = some w →
    CerberusHeapLang.toValRt { e := e, ρ := ρ } = some { w := w, ρ := ρ }
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toValRt_of_toVal_none :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack},
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none → CerberusHeapLang.toValRt { e := e, ρ := ρ } = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_action_node :
∀ (a : List annot) (p : generic_paction core_run_annotation Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction p)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_annot_cases :
∀ (a : List annot) (c : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ds : List dyn_annotation),
  (a = [] ∧
      ∃ v,
        c = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v) ∧
          CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds c)) =
            some (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) ∨
    CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds c)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_annot_none :
∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds e)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_ofVal :
∀ (v : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal), CerberusHeapLang.toVal (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal v) = some v
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_sseq_node :
∀ (a : List annot) (pat : pattern) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq pat e1 e2)) = none
----
def CerberusHeapLang.triple :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      Iris.IProp GF → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF) → Prop
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.triple_conseq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {P' P : Iris.IProp GF} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {Ψ Ψ' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  (P' ⊢ P) →
    (∀ (v : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal), Ψ v ⊢ Ψ' v) →
      CerberusHeapLang.triple P e Ψ → CerberusHeapLang.triple P' e Ψ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.triple_frame :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {P R : Iris.IProp GF} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  CerberusHeapLang.triple P e Ψ → CerberusHeapLang.triple iprop(P ∗ R) e fun v => iprop(Ψ v ∗ R)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.triple_seq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {P Q R : Iris.IProp GF} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  (CerberusHeapLang.triple P e1 fun x => Q) →
    (CerberusHeapLang.triple Q e2 fun x => R) →
      CerberusHeapLang.triple P (CerberusHeapLang.sseqExpr bty e1 e2) fun x => R
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.update_thread_state_single :
∀ (th th' : thread_state) (cs : core_state),
  cs.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    update_thread_state 0 th' cs = { thread_states := [(0, none, th')], io := cs.io }
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.valueFromPexpr_val :
∀ (a : List annot) (v : value), valueFromPexpr (Pexpr a () (PEval v)) = some v
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_annot :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (ds : List dyn_annotation) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {Φ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  WP { e := e, ρ := ρ } @ s ; E {{ v, Φ (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge ds v) }} ⊢
    WP { e := generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e), ρ := ρ } @ s ; E {{ Φ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_annot_reindex :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (a : List annot) (dsA dsB : List dyn_annotation)
  (c : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {Φ₁ Φ₂ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  (∀ (v : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal),
      Φ₁ (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge dsA v) = Φ₂ (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge dsB v)) →
    WP { e := generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot dsA c), ρ := ρ } @ s ; E {{ Φ₁ }} ⊢
      WP { e := generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot dsB c), ρ := ρ } @ s ; E {{ Φ₂ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_env_invariant :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {Φ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  WP { e := e, ρ := ρ } @ s ; E {{ Φ }} ⊢ WP { e := e, ρ := ρ } @ s ; E {{ v, ⌜v.ρ = ρ⌝ ∗ Φ v }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_load :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation)
  (ty : ctype) (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue) (mo : memory_order) (dq : Iris.DFrac)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.cellLoadTrap { addr := CerberusHeapLang.addrOf pv, ty := ty, bytes := bs } =
      false →
    pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs ⊢
      WP { e := CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann ty pv mo, ρ := ρ } @ s ;
        E {{
          w,
            ∃ fp,
              ⌜w =
                    {
                      w :=
                        CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp]
                          (CerberusHeapLang.loadedVal pv ty bs),
                      ρ := ρ }⌝ ∗
                pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_ofVal :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (v : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal)
  {Φ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  Φ v ⊢ WP (CerberusHeapLang.ofValRt v) @ s ; E {{ Φ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_sseq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (a pa : List annot) (bty : core_base_type)
  (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value))
  {Φ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  WP { e := e1, ρ := ev0 :: evs } @ s ;
      E {{ v, ▷ WP { e := e2, ρ := v.ρ } @ s ; E {{ w, Φ (CerberusHeapLang.mergeInto v w) }} }} ⊢
    WP
      { e := generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2),
        ρ := ev0 :: evs } @
      s ; E {{ Φ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_store :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation)
  (ty : ctype) (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue) (cv : value) (mo : memory_order) (mv : CerbMem.MemValue)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv →
    CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv →
      pv ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty ; bs ⊢
        WP { e := CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann ty pv cv mo, ρ := ρ } @ s ;
          E {{
            w,
              ∃ fp,
                ⌜w = { w := CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] Vunit, ρ := ρ }⌝ ∗
                  pv ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty ; (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd }}
----
def CerberusHeapLang.wps :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap →
        CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF →
          (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
            CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF
----
def CerberusHeapLang.wps.pre :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap →
        CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF →
          ((CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
              CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
            (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
              CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps.pre.contractive :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap) (Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF),
  Iris.OFE.Contractive (CerberusHeapLang.wps.pre Q Ls)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_annot :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF} (ds : List dyn_annotation)
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF},
  CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls (fun w ρ' => Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge ds w) ρ') e ρ ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_annot_reindex :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF} (a : List annot)
  (dsA dsB : List dyn_annotation) (c : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {Ψ₁ Ψ₂ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF},
  (∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
      Ψ₁ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge dsA w) ρ' =
        Ψ₂ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge dsB w) ρ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ₁ (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot dsA c)) ρ ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ₂ (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot dsB c)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_exhibit_seq_stores :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF} (x y : CerbMem.PointerValue)
  (loc loc' : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ann' : core_run_annotation) (mo mo' : memory_order)
  (bty : core_base_type) (bsx bsy : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bsx ∗
      y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bsy ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls
      (fun x_1 x_2 =>
        iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes ∗
            y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes))
      (CerberusHeapLang.sseqExpr bty
        (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy x CerberusHeapLang.fiveVal mo)
        (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc' ann' CerberusHeapLang.intTy y CerberusHeapLang.sixVal mo'))
      (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_exhibit_store_frame :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF} (x y : CerbMem.PointerValue)
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bs bs' : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ty' : ctype) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bs ∗ y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty' ; bs' ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls
      (fun x_1 x_2 =>
        iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes ∗
            y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty' ; bs'))
      (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy x CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_frame :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} {R : Iris.IProp GF}
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ e ρ ∗ R ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls (fun w ρ' => iprop(Ψ w ρ' ∗ R)) e ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_load :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype) (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue)
  (mo : memory_order) (dq : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.cellLoadTrap { addr := CerberusHeapLang.addrOf pv, ty := ty, bytes := bs } =
      false →
    (pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs ∗
        ∀ fp,
          pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs -∗
            Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] (CerberusHeapLang.loadedVal pv ty bs))
              ρ) ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann ty pv mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_ofVal :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  Ψ w ρ ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal w) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_seq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a pa : List annot)
  (bty : core_base_type) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls
      (fun w ρ' => CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls (fun u ρ'' => Ψ (w.mergeInto u) ρ'') e2 ρ') e1
      (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ
      (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_sound :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ e ρ ⊢
    WP { e := e, ρ := ρ } @ Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ; ⊤ {{ w, Ψ w.w w.ρ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_store :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype) (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue)
  (cv : value) (mo : memory_order) (mv : CerbMem.MemValue) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv →
    CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv →
      (pv ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty ; bs ∗
          ∀ fp,
            pv ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty ; (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd -∗
              Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] Vunit) ρ) ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann ty pv cv mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_unfold :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack},
  CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ e ρ ⊣⊢ CerberusHeapLang.wps.pre Q Ls (CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls) Ψ e ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_value_inv :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal w) ρ ⊢ |={⊤}=> Ψ w ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_wand :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ₁ Ψ₂ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ₁ e ρ ⊢
    (∀ w ρ', Ψ₁ w ρ' -∗ Ψ₂ w ρ') -∗ CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ₂ e ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.writeBytesTo_allocations :
∀ (st : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  (CerbMem.writeBytesTo st a bs).allocations = st.allocations
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.writeBytesTo_bytemap_get? :
∀ (st : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (k : Int),
  (CerbMem.writeBytesTo st a bs).bytemap.get? k =
    if a ≤ k ∧ k < a + ↑bs.length then bs[(k - a).toNat]? else st.bytemap.get? k
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.writeBytesTo_deadAllocations :
∀ (st : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  (CerbMem.writeBytesTo st a bs).deadAllocations = st.deadAllocations
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.writeBytesTo_funptrmap :
∀ (st : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  (CerbMem.writeBytesTo st a bs).funptrmap = st.funptrmap
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.writeBytesTo_lastUsedUnionMembers :
∀ (st : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  (CerbMem.writeBytesTo st a bs).lastUsedUnionMembers = st.lastUsedUnionMembers

theorem apply_ctx.eq_def :
∀ (ctx : context) (expr1 : generic_expr core_run_annotation Unit sym),
  apply_ctx ctx expr1 =
    match ctx with
    | CTX => expr1
    | Cunseq annot1 es1 ctx' es2 =>
      generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Eunseq (es1 ++ apply_ctx ctx' expr1 :: es2))
    | Cwseq annot1 pat ctx' e2 => generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Ewseq pat (apply_ctx ctx' expr1) e2)
    | Csseq annot1 pat ctx' e2 => generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Esseq pat (apply_ctx ctx' expr1) e2)
    | Cannot annot1 xs ctx' => generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Eannot xs (apply_ctx ctx' expr1))
    | Cbound annot1 ctx' => generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Ebound (apply_ctx ctx' expr1))
----
theorem assoc_adjust.eq_def :
∀ {a b : Type} [inst : Lem_Basic_classes.Eq0 a] (f : b → b) (k : a) (x : List (a × b)),
  assoc_adjust f k x =
    match x with
    | [] => []
    | (k', v) :: xs => if (k == k') = true then (k, f v) :: xs else (k', v) :: assoc_adjust f k xs
----
theorem drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ (lemFuel : Nat) (_lemReader_tagDefs : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)),
  drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs acc =
    match (motive :=
      Nat →
        List Nat →
          ndM (Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)) step_kind driver_error
            (mem_constraint CerbMem.IntegerValue) driver_state)
      lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted fun x => ND fun st => (NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown []), st)
    | lemFuel.succ => fun x =>
      match x with
      | [] => nd_return acc
      | tid1 :: xs' =>
        nd_bind
          (nd_read fun dr_st =>
            have th_info :=
              match Lem_List.lookupBy (fun x y => x == y) tid1 dr_st.core_state0.thread_states with
              | some z => z
              | none =>
                failwithI
                  ("Driver.drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2 => invalid tid: ".append
                    (Lem_String_extra.stringFromNat tid1));
            step_ctx _lemReader_tagDefs dr_st.layout_state dr_st.core_file dr_st.core_extern tid1
              th_info)
          fun steps =>
          match find_can_advance steps with
          | none =>
            drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs
              (fmapAddBy defaultCompare tid1 steps acc) xs'
          | some step1 =>
            nd_bind (advance_step _lemReader_tagDefs tid1 step1) fun wakeups =>
              have xs'' :=
                match wakeups with
                | NOWAKEUP => tid1 :: xs'
                | WAKEUP false tids => tid1 :: list_inserts tids xs'
                | WAKEUP true tids => list_inserts tids xs';
              drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs acc xs''
----
theorem driver2_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ (lemFuel : Nat) (_lemReader_tagDefs : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (with_concurrency : Bool),
  driver2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs with_concurrency =
    match lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted (ND fun st => (NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown []), st))
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      nd_bind (new_drive_core_threads _lemReader_tagDefs ()) fun tid_steps =>
        nd_bind nd_get fun post_core_dr_st =>
          have _non_blocked_th_sts :=
            List.filter
              (fun p =>
                match p with
                | (tid1, th_info) =>
                  (step_ctx _lemReader_tagDefs post_core_dr_st.layout_state
                        post_core_dr_st.core_file post_core_dr_st.core_extern tid1 th_info).any
                    fun step1 => step1 != Step_blocked2)
              post_core_dr_st.core_state0.thread_states;
          if
              Lem_Maybe.maybeEqualBy (fun x y => x == y) (CerbGlobal.current_execution_mode ())
                  (some CerbGlobal.ExecutionMode.random) =
                true then
            bindExhaustive (pick (SK_misc ["driver 2"]) tid_steps) fun p =>
              match p with
              | (tid1, step_opt) =>
                match step_opt with
                | some step1 =>
                  process_core_step2 _lemReader_tagDefs with_concurrency
                    (driver2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs) step1
                | none => nd_return ()
          else
            have non_blocked :=
              List.filter
                (fun p =>
                  match p with
                  | (tid1, step_opt) =>
                    !Lem_Maybe.maybeEqualBy (fun x y => x == y) step_opt (some Step_blocked2))
                tid_steps;
            nd_bind (if (non_blocked.length == 0) = true then nd_return () else nd_return ())
              fun u =>
              nd_bind (pick (SK_misc ["driver non_blocked"]) non_blocked) fun x =>
                match x with
                | (fst, none) => nd_return ()
                | (fst, some step1) =>
                  process_core_step2 _lemReader_tagDefs with_concurrency
                    (driver2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs) step1
----
theorem get_loc.eq_def :
∀ (annots1 : List annot),
  get_loc annots1 =
    match annots1 with
    | [] => none
    | Aloc loc1 :: tail => some loc1
    | Astd a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Auid a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Amarker a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Amarker_object_types a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Abmc a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Aattrs a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Atypedef a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Alabel a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Acerb a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Avalue a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Ainlined_label a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Astmt :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Aexpr :: annots' => get_loc annots'
----
theorem is_unseq_with_ccall_aux.eq_def :
∀ (acc : Bool) (x : context),
  is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc x =
    match x with
    | CTX => acc
    | Cunseq a es1 ctx' es2 =>
      have acc' := acc || (es1 ++ es2).any has_ccall;
      is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc' ctx'
    | Cwseq a a_1 ctx' e2 => is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc ctx'
    | Csseq a a_1 ctx' e2 => is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc ctx'
    | Cannot a a_1 ctx' => is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc ctx'
    | Cbound a ctx' => is_unseq_with_ccall_aux false ctx'
----
theorem liftAction_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ {a cs err1 err2 info1 info2 st1 st2 : Type} (lemFuel : Nat) (get2 : st2 → st1)
  (put1 : st2 → st1 → st2) (liftInfo : info1 → info2) (liftErr : err1 → err2),
  liftAction_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr =
    match (motive := Nat → nd_action a info1 err1 cs st1 → nd_action a info2 err2 cs st2)
      lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted fun x => NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown [])
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      match (motive :=
        (st2 → st1) →
          (st2 → st1 → st2) →
            (info1 → info2) →
              (err1 → err2) → nd_action a info1 err1 cs st1 → nd_action a info2 err2 cs st2)
        get2, put1, liftInfo, liftErr with
      | get2, put1, liftInfo, liftErr => fun x =>
        match x with
        | NDactive a1 => NDactive a1
        | NDkilled r =>
          NDkilled
            (match r with
            | Undef0 loc1 ubs => Undef0 loc1 ubs
            | Error0 loc1 str => Error0 loc1 str
            | Other err => Other (liftErr err))
        | NDnd info str_ms =>
          NDnd (liftInfo info)
            (List.map
              (fun p =>
                match p with
                | (info, m_act) =>
                  (liftInfo info, liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr m_act))
              str_ms)
        | NDguard info cs1 act =>
          NDguard (liftInfo info) cs1 (liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr act)
        | NDbranch info cs1 m_act1 m_act2 =>
          NDbranch (liftInfo info) cs1 (liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr m_act1)
            (liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr m_act2)
        | NDstep info info_ms =>
          NDstep (liftInfo info)
            (List.map
              (fun p =>
                match p with
                | (info, m) => (liftInfo info, liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr m))
              info_ms)
----
theorem liftND_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ {a cs err1 err2 info1 info2 st1 st2 : Type} (lemFuel : Nat) (get2 : st2 → st1)
  (put1 : st2 → st1 → st2) (liftInfo : info1 → info2) (liftErr : err1 → err2)
  (n : ndM a info1 err1 cs st1),
  liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr n =
    match lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted (ND fun st => (NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown []), st))
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      match get2, put1, liftInfo, liftErr, n with
      | get2, put1, liftInfo, liftErr, ND m =>
        ND fun st21 =>
          match m (get2 st21) with
          | (act, st1') =>
            (liftAction_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr act, put1 st21 st1')
----
theorem nd_bind_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ {a b c d e f : Type} (lemFuel : Nat) (n : ndM f b d a c) (f1 : f → ndM e b d a c),
  nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel n f1 =
    match lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted (ND fun st => (NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown []), st))
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      match n, f1 with
      | ND m_act, f1 =>
        ND fun st =>
          match m_act st with
          | (NDactive z, st') =>
            match f1 z with
            | ND m_act' => m_act' st'
          | (NDkilled r, st') => (NDkilled r, st')
          | (NDnd debug_str str_ms, st') =>
            (NDnd debug_str
                (List.map
                  (fun p =>
                    match p with
                    | (str, m) => (str, nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m f1))
                  str_ms),
              st')
          | (NDguard debug_str cs m_act', st') =>
            (NDguard debug_str cs (nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m_act' f1), st')
          | (NDbranch debug_str cs m_act1 m_act2, st') =>
            (NDbranch debug_str cs (nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m_act1 f1)
                (nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m_act2 f1),
              st')
          | (NDstep info str_ms, st') =>
            (NDstep info
                (List.map
                  (fun p =>
                    match p with
                    | (str, m) => (str, nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m f1))
                  str_ms),
              st')
----
